Returns removed element count.\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n\n    # Build parent map scoped to this paragraph (avoid holding full doc mapping)\n    parent: dict[ET.Element, ET.Element] = {}\n    for par in p_elem.iter():\n        for child in list(par):\n            parent[child] = par\n\n    # Remove deepest-first so child removals don't break parent iteration\n    all_desc = list(p_elem.iter())\n    for elem in reversed(all_desc):\n        ln = localname(elem.tag)\n        if ln.lower() in LAYOUT_CACHE_LOCALNAMES:\n            par = parent.get(elem)\n            if par is not None:\n                try:\n                    par.remove(elem)\n                    removed += 1\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n    return removed\n\n\ndef extract_template() -> None:\n    if os.path.exists(WORK_DIR):\n        shutil.rmtree(WORK_DIR)\n    os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as z:\n        z.extractall(WORK_DIR)\n\n\ndef find_placeholders_in_xml_files(xml_paths: list[str]) -> set[str]:\n    found: set[str] = set()\n    for p in xml_paths:\n        with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            s = f.read()\n        for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(s):\n            found.add(m.group(1).strip())\n    return found\n\n\ndef fill_xml_file(path: str, value_map: dict[str, str]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fill a single XML file. Returns summary dict.\"\"\"\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        raw = f.read()\n\n    if \"{{\" not in raw and \"}}\" not in raw:\n        return {\n            \"file\": path,\n            \"placeholders\": [],\n            \"paragraphs_modified\": 0,\n            \"layout_cache_removed\": 0,\n        }\n\n    tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(raw))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    file_placeholders = sorted(set(m.group(1).strip() for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(raw)))\n\n    paragraphs_modified = 0\n    layout_cache_removed = 0\n\n    # ZIP validity\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"r\") as z:\n        bad = z.testzip()\n        if bad is not None:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"ZIP validation failed; first bad file: {bad}\")\n\n        xml_names = [n for n in z.namelist() if n.lower().endswith(\".xml\")]\n        all_xml = {}\n        for name in xml_names:\n            all_xml[name] = z.read(name).decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n\n    # No placeholders remain\n    leftovers = [(n, s.count(\"{{\"), s.count(\"}}\")) for n, s in all_xml.items() if \"{{\" in s or \"}}\" in s]\n    if leftovers:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Found unreplaced placeholders in output: {leftovers}\")\n\n    # 참석자수 digits only check (the replaced text should contain '참석자 수: <digits>')\n    attendee_expected = summary[\"value_map\"].get(\"참석자수\", \"\")\n    if attendee_expected and not re.fullmatch(r\"\\d+\", attendee_expected):\n        raise SystemExit(f\"참석자수 is not digits-only after formatting: {attendee_expected!r}\")\n\n    # We only require they be removed from modified paragraphs, but template is tiny; enforce none remain.\n        # This is conservative to prevent overlap issues.\n        if cache_re.search(s):\n            cache_hits.append(n)\n    if cache_hits:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Layout cache nodes still present in output XML: {cache_hits}\")\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with open(JSON_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    extract_template()\n\n    xml_paths = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(WORK_DIR, \"**\", \"*.xml\"), recursive=True))\n    placeholders = find_placeholders_in_xml_files(xml_paths)\n    value_map = build_value_map(data, placeholders)\n\n    per_file = []\n    for p in xml_paths:\n        per_file.append(fill_xml_file(p, value_map))\n\n    rezip()\n\n    summary = {\n        \"placeholders_found\": sorted(placeholders),\n        \"value_map\": value_map,\n        \"per_file\": per_file,\n        \"output\": OUT_PATH,\n    }\n\n    validate_output(summary)\n\n    # Append exactly with a leading space before '후속'\n    return base + \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n\n\ndef build_value_map(data: dict, placeholders: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:\n    out: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for ph in sorted(placeholders):\n        if ph == \"참석자수\":\n            out[ph] = digits_only(data.get(ph))\n        elif ph == \"만족도\":\n            out[ph] = format_satisfaction(data.get(ph))\n        elif ph == \"종합의견\":\n            out[ph] = format_overall_opinion(data.get(ph))\n        else:\n            v = data.get(ph)\n            out[ph] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n    return out\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    # {namespace}name -> name\n    return tag.rsplit(\"}\", 1)[-1] if \"}\" in tag else tag\n\n\n# Layout-cache nodes that must be removed after text edits.\n# We compare by *localname* (namespace stripped) and lowercase.\nLAYOUT_CACHE_LOCALNAMES = {\n    \"linesegarray\",\n    \"lineseg\",\n    \"linesegarrays\",\n    \"linesegarray\".lower(),\n    \"lineseg\".lower(),\n    \"linesegarrays\".lower(),\n    \"linesegarray\".lower(),\n    \"lineseg\".lower(),\n    \"lineSegArray\".lower(),\n    \"lineSeg\".lower(),\n}\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_elem: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove layout-cache nodes from a paragraph element. Returns removed element count.\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n\n    # Build parent map scoped to this paragraph (avoid holding full doc mapping)\n    parent: dict[ET.Element, ET.Element] = {}\n    for par in p_elem.iter():\n        for child in list(par):\n            parent[child] = par\n\n    # Remove deepest-first so child removals don't break parent iteration\n    all_desc = list(p_elem.iter())\n    for elem in reversed(all_desc):\n        ln = localname(elem.tag)\n        if ln.lower() in LAYOUT_CACHE_LOCALNAMES:\n            par = parent.get(elem)\n            if par is not None:\n                try:\n                    par.remove(elem)\n                    removed += 1\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n    return removed\n\n\ndef extract_template() -> None:\n    if os.path.exists(WORK_DIR):\n        shutil.rmtree(WORK_DIR)\n    os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as z:\n        z.extractall(WORK_DIR)\n\n\ndef find_placeholders_in_xml_files(xml_paths: list[str]) -> set[str]:\n    found: set[str] = set()\n    for p in xml_paths:\n        with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            s = f.read()\n        for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(s):\n            found.add(m.group(1).strip())\n    return found\n\n\ndef fill_xml_file(path: str, value_map: dict[str, str]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fill a single XML file. Returns summary dict.\"\"\"\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        raw = f.read()\n\n    if \"{{\" not in raw and \"}}\" not in raw:\n        return {\n            \"file\": path,\n            \"placeholders\": [],\n            \"paragraphs_modified\": 0,\n            \"layout_cache_removed\": 0,\n        }\n\n    tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(raw))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    file_placeholders = sorted(set(m.group(1).strip() for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(raw)))\n\n    paragraphs_modified = 0\n    layout_cache_removed = 0\n\n    # Iterate paragraphs and replace inside text runs\n    for p_elem in root.iter():\n        if localname(p_elem.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        changed = False\n        for t_elem in p_elem.iter():\n            if localname(t_elem.tag) != \"t\":\n                continue\n            if t_elem.text is None:\n                continue\n\n            old_text = t_elem.text\n            new_text = old_text\n\n            # Replace all placeholders present in this text node\n            for ph in PLACEHOLDER_RE.findall(old_text):\n                ph_key = ph.strip()\n                if ph_key not in value_map:\n                    continue\n                new_text = re.sub(r\"\\{\\{\\s*\" + re.escape(ph_key) + r\"\\s*\\}\\}\", value_map[ph_key], new_text)\n\n            if new_text != old_text:\n                t_elem.text = new_text\n                changed = True\n\n        if changed:\n            paragraphs_modified += 1\n            layout_cache_removed += remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_elem)\n\n    # Write back\n    new_raw = ET.tostring(root, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True).decode(\"utf-8\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(new_raw)\n\n    return {\n        \"file\": path,\n        \"placeholders\": file_placeholders,\n        \"paragraphs_modified\": paragraphs_modified,\n        \"layout_cache_removed\": layout_cache_removed,\n    }\n\n\ndef rezip() -> None:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(WORK_DIR):\n            for fn in files:\n                full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(full, WORK_DIR)\n                zout.write(full, arcname)\n\n\ndef validate_output(summary: dict) -> None:\n    # ZIP validity\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"r\") as z:\n        bad = z.testzip()\n        if bad is not None:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"ZIP validation failed; first bad file: {bad}\")\n\n        xml_names = [n for n in z.namelist() if n.lower().endswith(\".xml\")]\n        all_xml = {}\n        for name in xml_names:\n            all_xml[name] = z.read(name).decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n\n    # No placeholders remain\n    leftovers = [(n, s.count(\"{{\"), s.count(\"}}\")) for n, s in all_xml.items() if \"{{\" in s or \"}}\" in s]\n    if leftovers:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Found unreplaced placeholders in output: {leftovers}\")\n\n    # 참석자수 digits only check (the replaced text should contain '참석자 수: <digits>')\n    attendee_expected = summary[\"value_map\"].get(\"참석자수\", \"\")\n    if attendee_expected and not re.fullmatch(r\"\\d+\", attendee_expected):\n        raise SystemExit(f\"참석자수 is not digits-only after formatting: {attendee_expected!r}\")\n\n    # 만족도 format check\n    sat_expected = summary[\"value_map\"].get(\"만족도\", \"\")\n    if sat_expected and not re.fullmatch(r\"\\d+\\.\\d점 \\(5\\.0점 만점\\)\", sat_expected):\n        raise SystemExit(f\"만족도 has wrong format: {sat_expected!r}\")\n\n    # 종합의견 suffix check\n    overall_expected = summary[\"value_map\"].get(\"종합의견\", \"\")\n    if overall_expected and not overall_expected.endswith(\"후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"):\n        raise SystemExit(\"종합의견 does not end with required suffix\")\n\n    #
